# SmartFlow AI — Program Blueprint

Runtime Extension 0.15.530 จับคู่กับ Desktop launcher 0.15.530.0; root EXE และ Extension ID เดิมเชื่อมต่อและรายงาน compatible แล้ว ผลจาก provider ยังต้องตรวจแยกต่างหาก

## Durable Story image audit / 0.15.530

ก่อนกดส่งภาพ ChatGPT Extension ต้องได้รับ ACK ว่า Bridge บันทึก `image_prompt_ready` ลง trace ของ Job แล้ว การตอบแบบ buffered, ignored, timeout หรือ transport error ไม่อนุญาตให้กดส่ง; ลองยืนยันซ้ำได้อีกหนึ่งครั้งโดยไม่แตะ provider. คำสั่ง Start ซ้ำของ Job และ run เดียวกันรับสถานะ collector ที่ทำงานอยู่ ส่วน run อื่นยังปฏิเสธ. งาน 7A98CB หยุดก่อน Send ในรอบ 529 และคิวพักไว้จนยืนยันเจ้าของร่างได้.

## Idle Story pre-Send reader / 0.15.529

After 528 prepared an image prompt, the three-minute watchdog found the same tab and durable pre-Send receipt but the content worker no longer owned its run. The recovery probe may now read the exact conversation from an idle content reader. Background still requires the same job, run, scene, registered tab, prepared receipt with no nonce or dispatch marker, and two stable page probes. Each probe checks the conversation URL, user-turn count and signature, absence of the request or active response, exact draft and source attachments. A different active owner, cancellation, changed page or any uncertain Send stays in review. Claiming the document fences new Starts until its exact tab reload completes.

## MAIN-world inline tool preflight / 0.15.528

The trusted Send controller compares the full wrapped wire prompt against editable text only, excluding an exact non-editable Create image token inside the ChatGPT composer. Missing wrapper text or a changed editable draft still vetoes the click. The 527 audited first-image prompt followed by `gesture_phase:not_started` and `preflight_reason:draft_mismatch` is a bounded pre-Send proof for the saved prepared receipt; a later image Send or unknown state invalidates it.

## Inline image-tool suffix and checkpoint tab continuation / 0.15.527

The ChatGPT Send preflight reads editable composer text and excludes a non-editable Create image token. This keeps the required single-answer suffix visible when the tool token renders after it; an actually missing suffix still blocks Send. A Story package with a saved analysis checkpoint resumes the registered job tab, including its unsent draft, instead of treating a repeated Open command as a fresh root. The exact 526 `image_prompt_ready` plus adjacent `AI_SEND_NOT_READY` and `AI_RESPONSE_FORMAT_NOT_READY` trace authorizes skipping the legacy whole-chat image scan and recovering only a prepared receipt. Unknown or accepted dispatch remains review-only.

## Latest first-image pre-Send proof / 0.15.526

If a previously resumed first image stops again before dispatch, the Story package chooses the latest exact `AI_SEND_NOT_READY` image-tool failure. The adjacent attempt, run, page, safe intervening trace, no accepted result and bounded receipt creation window must match. This permits skipping legacy conversation scanning and transferring only that latest prepared receipt; an accepted or uncertain Send remains protected by both trace checks and the scene receipt's durable dispatch check.

## Canonical owned draft for image-tool recheck / 0.15.525

The ChatGPT post-insertion tool check receives the canonical prompt because `composerText()` strips the single-answer transport instruction from the editor. Passing `wrap(prompt)` made the Extension reject its own inserted draft before Send. Story image, visual repair and cover use the same canonical comparison. A foreign or changed draft still stops before Send, and failure diagnostics record only structural lengths and node types, not prompt content.

## Proven prepared receipt transfer / 0.15.524

The exact 521 first-image no-dispatch trace also binds the old prepared receipt to its run, Extension ID, original conversation and creation time. If a later resume opens a different ChatGPT conversation, the content worker may release only that pre-Send receipt when no durable dispatch value, send nonce, result proof, image, active generation or foreign draft exists. The next `begin()` writes the current run's receipt before a new Send. Missing or conflicting evidence still stops at `STORY_IMAGE_RECEIPT_REVIEW`.

## Proven first-image pre-Send recovery / 0.15.523

When a saved Story analysis has no image checkpoint, a resumed ChatGPT run ordinarily checks legacy images in the whole conversation. The exact 0.15.521 first-image tool-preflight failure proves no image Send occurred only when the same run has the adjacent non-retryable attempt and `dispatch_completed=false`, no previous image attempt, and no later image work. The Story package exposes scoped proof for that case. The Extension skips the page-wide legacy scan and continues to the scene receipt, which still checks any uncertain or accepted request before a new Send. Missing or conflicting proof keeps ordinary recovery behavior.

## Inline ChatGPT image-tool draft guard / 0.15.522

After Story analysis is saved, ChatGPT may render a selected Create image token inside the editor; its label must not be treated as user draft text. Only an exact non-editable UI token is excluded, and an editable user draft still stops before Send. The image tool is rechecked after Story image, visual repair and AI cover prompt insertion because replacing editor contents can drop that tool selection. No accepted or uncertain provider Send is replayed; the exact failed Story remains paused until its saved analysis and image pre-Send ownership are verified.

## Owned browser surface recheck before Story Send / 0.15.521

After a trusted hover reaches the exact composer-owned Send target, Background checks that the same Chrome window remains visible and the owned tab stays active before writing the durable dispatch claim. It restores one changed surface, rechecks owner, readiness and target geometry, then requires a fresh trusted hover. A second change or missing tab stops before press with `send_surface_changed`; accepted and uncertain Sends are never replayed.

## Late AI cover acceptance / 0.15.521

After an uncertain cover Send, the content worker observes the same owned user turn and references for at most three minutes. Three stable acceptance observations continue to the existing image collector without another click or upload. If acceptance remains uncertain, the request stays in review; the worker does not retry a dispatched Send. The previous unsent-draft retry remains available only when dispatch did not occur.

## Desktop notification layout / 2026-10-07

The shared `#notification-stack` owns bottom-right placement for transient toasts, minimized creation/posting progress and the version/update control. Its flex layout reserves space only for visible notices, constrains long progress text to the viewport and keeps the update dialog in the document body. `web_ui/notification_stack.css` loads after other component styles. Verify with `--feature notification-layout`; an active customer queue delays normal DEV window restart and activation proof.

## Direct Story Continue and stopped-work display (2026-10-07)

The Continue page displays recoverable jobs above the queue. Within the queue, running work comes first, then failed or cancelled rows still bound to a Job, then untouched queued rows; this is display order only and does not change the durable FIFO. A direct Story checkpoint retry now claims its exact failed queue row as running before starting work, while preserving the previous error and paused queue. It refuses cancelled ownership or another running row. The normal Story ready/error finisher can then record the recovered result against that row. A completed Story belongs in the library and must not remain a stopped queue item.

## ChatGPT Send input delivery / 0.15.519

The Background first resolves the unique Send button from the exact composer form and checks the full wire draft, viewport hit point, owner, and response state. CDP may acknowledge `mouseMoved` or press/release without delivering events to the page. Before persisting a Story dispatch claim or pressing Send, the Extension now requires a trusted `mousemove` observed at the same target point in the page. If absent, it focuses the owned window/tab once, rechecks readiness and target, and probes again. A second miss returns `input_not_delivered` with `gesture_phase=not_started`, preserving the draft and attachment for bounded recovery. After a press, the existing uncertain-send rule still forbids another Send. The 518 scene-2 receipt remains uncertain and cannot be retroactively replayed by this guard.

## Story image pre-Send stall recovery / 0.15.518

The Extension bounds the mandatory `image_prompt_ready` ACK to 12 seconds. A timed-out ACK stops before any provider Send and allows the existing checkpoint recovery; an explicit rejected ACK stays in review. If an acknowledged pre-Send step remains unchanged for three minutes, the Desktop queues one exact-run guarded recovery. The Extension requires a contiguous saved-scene checkpoint, matching prepared receipt and owned tab, no dispatch claim, and two live DOM checks for the unchanged draft, attachments, response state and absence of the exact user turn. The second check claims the old content worker before any click; the Extension rechecks the receipt, reloads only that owned tab, and resumes the missing scene in the same conversation. If proof changes or recovery has no progress after 90 seconds, the FIFO stops with an explicit review reason and retains media and chat. The active 517 Story E3C5C5 has five saved images but no 518 audit receipt, so its old scene-6 draft is not automatically replayed or reloaded by this candidate.

## Pre-Send Story bootstrap resume / 0.15.517

A `story_bootstrap_review` emitted by Background before `START_CHATGPT_JOB` proves that run did not reach provider Send. Resume may start the same failed Job fresh only when its complete Job trace contains solely matching bootstrap reviews and routing errors, no valid conversation URL, no provider progress/Send evidence, no analysis checkpoint, no saved images, and no owner-bearing resume checkpoint. A stale `job_trace` analysis hint with an empty URL is ignored and removed as that Job is marked running. Any other trace or owner proof retains the read-only old-conversation route. The Error Log labels a saved prompt as prepared unless an analysis `ai_send_accepted` event exists. This repairs Story E3C5C5's 516 retry without creating a new Job or replaying an accepted request.

## Story bootstrap attachment and late-draft guard / 0.15.516

Before opening a new Story, the Extension checks the exact ChatGPT root document for a draft, conversation, response, or actual file preview/input. A Create image tool icon and generic busy indicator are not upload evidence. A draft restored into a newly created tab has unknown ownership, so the Extension preserves it and tries one other new root. It checks the same document again after content-script injection and before Story Start. Any late draft or attachment stops with a bounded, content-free `AI_WEB_WAIT_REVIEW` reason before provider Send. The content script's image-tool guard also rejects a draft that differs from the request being prepared. Story E3C5C5's failed first attempt had no provider Send; resuming it requires installed 516 and a verified clean root.

## Owner-supplied recovery of an accepted AI cover (2026-10-05)

When the provider has accepted a cover request but the result reader cannot verify the original attachment labels after the reply renders, the queue remains paused. `AICovers.adopt_user_supplied_result` can save an original cover file supplied by the owner against that exact latest request without another provider Send. It requires a terminal review state with accepted or uncertain Send evidence, a ready video, unchanged cover revision, a valid image and the requested orientation. It records `completion_source=user_supplied` and a request-scoped cover history entry; it does not claim the Extension collected or verified the image. The source file and finished video remain untouched. The queue's existing completion gate opens only after the request-owned copy is saved. This path is used only with an explicitly supplied image file; a screenshot of queue status is not image evidence.

## Story Shorts Quick Setup scroll and progress (2026-10-05)

The new Story form's Quick Setup is a bounded scroll panel with three visible sections: image/style, video/scenes, and review/reference image. Its navigation and progress indicate the section currently in view; selecting a section scrolls within the settings panel without changing form values or starting a job. The existing Story creation payload and provider guards remain unchanged.

## Story bootstrap occupied fresh root (0.15.510, 2026-10-05)

Before a new Story analysis request, an owned ChatGPT root that restores an attachment or conversation is preserved. The Extension can try one other new root, then requires a stable empty document before Start. A second occupied root produces a bounded review with a specific reason; the failed job and all prior provider state remain untouched. Source and fixture verification do not prove installed activation or provider output.

## ChatGPT post-attach Send recheck / 0.15.509

The initial Send target may be ready, then disappear while Chrome debugger attaches or the composer rerenders. Before any physical press, Background rechecks `send_not_ready`, `response_active`, `target_blocked`, or `send_target_ambiguous` at most three times total, five seconds apart. This includes the final read after the Story dispatch latch is written, within the same handler and before a press. Each probe revalidates the exact job, run, tab, receipt and full draft. Changed ownership or draft stops immediately. A final failure records `preflight_stage=after_attach` or `after_claim`, reason and count without prompt content, and preserves saved scenes. A later attempt cannot replay a claimed, accepted or uncertain Send. This covers Story `STORY-20261005-7B9E37` scene 2 after Extension 507's first-stage retry did not apply.

## Owner-authorized uncertain Story image replay / 0.15.505–507

## Prior-run Story image draft watchdog / 0.15.504

Checkpoint Resume may enter result-only recovery with a `dispatching` image receipt from a previous run. When the owned ChatGPT request is missing, the exact original prompt remains in the composer, and no response is active, the monitor now makes three read-only observations spanning at least 15 seconds. If unchanged, it reports `image_send_stalled` and raises `STORY_IMAGE_RECEIPT_REVIEW` with `PRIOR_RUN_UNCONFIRMED_DRAFT_PRESENT`. The receipt, source image and saved scenes remain; no refresh or Send is attempted. A late matching request or image remains eligible for the existing result-only path. This fixes the separate Resume route found when the 503 candidate was activated and Story 55A401 was retried from scene 1.

## Uncertain Story image Send watchdog / 0.15.503

The Extension bridge heartbeat reports connection and job status, but it cannot prove that ChatGPT accepted a clicked Send. In installed 502, Story 55A401 scene 2 showed a dispatched gesture, an unchanged prompt with a reference image in the composer, no matching user turn, and `request_missing` for several minutes. The result monitor could not safely reload an occupied composer and kept waiting. After the existing one-minute Send acceptance window, 503 checks the same draft and exact job/run three more times at five-second intervals. If the draft and missing request remain unchanged with no active response, it reports `image_send_stalled` and raises `STORY_IMAGE_RECEIPT_REVIEW` with `SEND_UNCONFIRMED_DRAFT_PRESENT`. This preserves the receipt, draft, attachment and saved scenes and pauses the queue without another Send. If a matching user turn appears during the checks, result-only recovery continues. Focused fixtures are not evidence that the running 502 content script has changed; activate only after the active job is idle.

## ChatGPT ambiguous Send recheck / 0.15.502

Version 501 saved scene 3 after one accepted Send but scene 4 stopped before a press with the generic unready message and without a delayed-recheck suffix. The exact Background reason was lost by the Story error wrapper; the code path suggests `send_target_ambiguous`. Version 502 adds that transient ambiguity to the same three read-only checks at five-second intervals. It never chooses among two live buttons or presses until one exact composer-owned target remains; changed draft, owner or cancellation still stops. Both successful Send diagnostics and prepress error reports keep only bounded reason enums and the recheck count, with no prompt or browser secret. Focused fixtures cover late uniqueness, persistent ambiguity with zero presses, and safe trace transport. Connected 502 saved scene 4 after one accepted Send and advanced to scene 5 without exercising the delayed path. An existing active tab may retain the prior content script until its next safe reload; the Background retry logic is active, and the tab must not be reloaded mid-job.

## ChatGPT Send readiness retry / 0.15.501

The Background prepress check now waits five seconds and rechecks at most three times when the exact owned ChatGPT composer has no ready Send control, a prior response is still active, or the control is temporarily blocked. Each probe checks the job/run owner and the full expected draft. A changed draft, ambiguous target, changed owner, or cancelled job stops immediately. The delay is entirely before the first physical press; no prompt rewrite, new tab, additional Send gesture, or accepted/uncertain receipt replay is allowed. A final failure reports the bounded recheck count and reasons and retains the prepared unsent Story receipt. The 501 fixture proves a late control can recover with one gesture and changed drafts still produce zero gestures. A real checkpoint retry under connected 501 sent scene 3 once, saved it, and advanced to scene 4; the five-second branch itself was not triggered in that run.

## Isolated WebView2 provider lab (`feature/webview2-prototype`, 2026-10-02)

`desktop/provider_prototype.py` remains a separate CLI research entry point with an isolated profile and read-only page probe. The source DEV MODE SmartFlow window has an `AI Chat` menu/page. `desktop/embedded_provider.py` adds a raw WebView2 child control to that same WinForms window, aligns it with the page host, and hides it on page exit. Its profile is separate from Chrome and the SmartFlow shell. The raw provider control has no pywebview JavaScript bridge, so ChatGPT cannot call `UpdateApi`. The page and bridge are gated to an unfrozen DEV checkout; the packaged customer app continues to use Chrome and its Extension for Story. A host-owned manual text test in `desktop/webview2_request.py` can send one request after login and a clean-composer check. It writes an atomic receipt before the single Send, verifies a new matching user turn, and requires a stable assistant turn. Unknown Send or result leaves `needs_review` and blocks replay; only IDs, the owned conversation path, hashes, counts and phases persist, never the prompt or response. A third distinct live request verified one Send and exact reply `WEBVIEW2_OK_3` automatically. This text-only test does not enter Story routing, upload images, save Story checkpoints, or replace the Extension. Native fixture smoke showed the provider in the SmartFlow window; customer package integration and Story completion remain unverified. See `docs/plans/webview2-provider-prototype.md`.

## Prior-run accepted Story image request (0.15.498 installed, 2026-10-02)

An empty ChatGPT response after refreshing the saved conversation does not prove that an accepted request from a cancelled earlier run failed. If the pending image receipt has an accepted or uncertain Send from a different run ID, result recovery retains the receipt and conversation and stops with `STORY_IMAGE_RECEIPT_REVIEW` before any reminder, new tab, or provider Send. The user can inspect the original conversation; no duplicate request is silently issued. Installed 497 did issue a second scene-6 request after an empty post-refresh result during Story E25264 Continue. Paired 498 is installed and connected; this exact guard has fixture evidence but no live provider result yet.

## Story image result under a stale progress marker (0.15.497 installed, 2026-10-02)

For an accepted Story image request, the content script still resolves the exact conversation, request turn and owned image. A full-size completed image with current-turn completion controls can be collected after its image and progress marker remain unchanged for 60 seconds, provided no Stop button is visible. This is result-only recovery; it neither sends a new prompt nor presses Stop. A changing image/marker or visible Stop continues to block collection. Trace details distinguish `stop_visible`, `progress_count` and `stale_progress`. The installed 496 live job E25264 showed `image_ready` but `response_active=true` at scene 5; its exact busy signal was not measured. Scene 5 saved at the cancellation handoff and scene 6 later saved under 497, but the 497 cross-run retry duplicated the scene-6 request.

## Story image composer scope and attachment count (0.15.496, 2026-10-02)

The ChatGPT retained-reference check, attachment counter and uploader now resolve the same composer shell, including the unified composer form when the editor is not nested in a form. A prepared unsent Story image can reuse its one named previous-scene attachment without another upload. Before any Story reference-image Send, the audit requires exactly the requested attachment count and no busy or failed upload; excess references stop before Send with a logged context conflict. The 495 live run produced a ready ten-scene Story and local video, but scene 5 had two attachment nodes for one source and therefore does not prove correct reuse. Paired 496 is installed and connected; this specific branch is not yet live-proven.

## ChatGPT composer whitespace in retained Story requests (0.15.495, 2026-10-02)

ChatGPT may render line breaks in an unsent image prompt as spaces. The retained-reference guard compares the entire wrapped prompt and live composer after collapsing whitespace, rather than raw character equality. It still requires the prepared unsent receipt, current run, same conversation, exactly one attachment bearing the generated previous-scene filename, no busy upload and no matching sent request. A changed non-whitespace character still stops before Send. The 494 live scene-5 stop was before Send and preserved images 1–4. Version 495 is paired and connected in DEV; live provider output remains unverified.

## Prepared Story image attachment recovery (0.15.494, 2026-10-02)

The Extension keeps a prepared receipt for an image request that failed before Send. On Resume it may use the same retained previous-scene attachment in the same ChatGPT conversation after checking the receipt, exact filename marker, one composer attachment, exact wrapped prompt and absence of a matching sent user turn. It then continues the existing request without a second upload. Any ownership or page-state mismatch remains a protected stop; an accepted or uncertain Send is never replayed. The paired 494 DEV desktop and original Chrome Extension ID are connected; the next actual Story Resume remains the live behavior check.

## UI foundation: shared palette, one status vocabulary, grouped sidebar (2026-10-07)

- Presentation only. No route, endpoint, action name, Job schema, checkpoint, receipt or Send-replay rule changed. All 22 pages keep their routes.
- `web_ui/foundation.css` loads after every other stylesheet. It holds the single colour token set: the `styles.css` tokens (`--bg`, `--cyan`, ...) and the `ux_2026.css` aliases (`--sf-*`) now share one palette. It also holds the status pill styles, a solid (non-gradient) primary button, and the grouped sidebar.
- `web_ui/status_vocabulary.js` (`window.SmartFlowStatus`) is the only place that maps backend status codes to what the customer reads. Six states: `รอคิว`, `กำลังสร้าง`, `รอคุณ`, `สะดุด`, `หยุดไว้`, `เสร็จแล้ว`. A few narrower codes keep their own label (`พร้อม`, `โพสต์แล้ว`, `ยกเลิกแล้ว`, `พร้อมส่ง AI`, login/credit waits). `สะดุด · ทำต่อได้` appears only where the page really offers a resume button (non-drama queue rows, Story recovery cards, Product continue cards).
- It must load before `app.js` and `creation_queue.js`. Any test harness that executes `creation_queue.js` must load it first.
- `SmartFlowStatus.pauseReason()` explains why the queue paused itself, using the same reason keys as `story_queue.pause(...)`. `tests/test_ui_foundation.py` fails when the backend adds a pause reason without customer text.
- Sidebar: `เผยแพร่`, `ตัวละครและตกแต่ง` and `ตั้งค่า` are `<details class="nav-group">`. The group that holds the active page opens, and the active item is scrolled into view. Voice, subtitle, audio, logo and guide, which used to be reachable only from buttons inside other pages, now have menu entries. Ten rows fit the default 1360x860 window without scrolling.
- Text: no `font-size` below 10px in any stylesheet (155 declarations of 7-9px were raised to 11px). Status pills are 12px and meet 4.5:1 contrast.
- Not covered: about 640 hard-coded colours remain in older stylesheets; slide-style create flow and first-run setup belong to later phases.

## งานของฉัน: คิว งานเดิม และโปรเจกต์ละคร (2026-10-07)

The existing `creation` route now presents `งานของฉัน`. `creation_queue.js` reads the existing queue, ordinary Story records, and drama-series snapshot for one filtered list. Queue rows retain their IDs and existing actions; their one visible primary action follows the current state, while move, remove, logs and cover tools sit in a disclosure. An old Story or drama row opens its existing recovery/project surface. No backend state or queue order changes. The active queue row remains first, stopped rows precede untouched queued rows, and scene counts come from each saved queue item. Queue pause text and all new status pills use `SmartFlowStatus`. Raw queue errors are not printed on the job card. This is source-only UI work; activation in the owner’s open DEV window awaits an idle restart.

## New-job slide wizard (2026-10-07)

`create_wizard.js` opens six steps from `งานของฉัน` for Product, Story, Drama and long video. It reads and writes the existing form controls, dispatching `input` and `change`; the last button clicks the existing submit button. The desktop action and payload builders remain in `app.js` and their wrapper stack. The original forms remain available for advanced settings. The wizard validates a Shopee link, a required title and speaking/video compatibility, uses the original scene and episode limits, and displays only actual system readiness fields. Provider web login is guidance, not a claimed state. Drama retains its one explicit enqueue button; `queue_choice.js` no longer adds a duplicate drama queue checkbox. `generation_notice.js` uses wording for the selected action type. Browser fixtures check field synchronization and the unchanged Story payload through the actual legacy click handler.

## Settings, first-run guidance and recovery presentation (2026-10-07)

The loaded desktop pages use the existing `#confirm-modal` for destructive and external actions; `window.smartflowConfirm` resolves the same user decision for modules that formerly called native browser `confirm()`. It does not change action names, request payloads, or provider-send ownership. General error toasts pass through `safeUiError`, while raw automation reports remain available only inside the existing support details. Technical English labels and route headings in the legacy pages are presentation text only, Android UI capture sits in a support disclosure, and the progress display shares Thai phase names across pipeline and fallback views.

The settings page keeps its current controls. `#setting-subtitle` in quick settings and `#subtitle-auto` in subtitle style both read the same backend `self.subtitle_auto` Boolean (`state.settings.subtitle_auto` / `state.subtitle.auto`). They are two entry points for the default for future work; the per-job subtitle control is a separate snapshot in each creation payload. The UI explains those scopes without merging controls or changing saved values. `setup_wizard.js` adds a seven-step guide and a dashboard 0–4 technical readiness count from extension compatibility, voice key, voice reference and subtitle connection. Provider login is a user confirmation only, never a system-verified claim.

The existing automation error dialog now shows three recovery guidance steps. Internal codes and log text remain inside `รายละเอียดสำหรับทีมช่วยเหลือ`; the visible title and summary are generic Thai. `recovery_wizard.js` maps known login, Flow terms/credits, draft, refusal, Extension mismatch and uncertain-send cases to safe instructions. It only opens existing web/Extension entry points and never sends to a provider. An uncertain prior Send has no replay button because no UI-bound owner-authorized replay contract was found; the original job, receipt and browser state remain untouched. Queue rows carrying such a code open this guidance instead of offering direct Retry. Flow-credit guidance offers only switching to a credited Google account and continuing with Flow.
## Story recovery status in the desktop UI (2026-10-02)

The Story Shorts recovery cards use `core/story_recovery_summary.py` and saved local scene files to show confirmed analysis, each saved image scene, the exact failed image scene, and later pending scenes separately. A job-level error no longer colors the whole card red. The last error is expandable; a `CHATGPT_IMAGE_RESULT_SEND_NOT_STARTED` scene is labeled as not sent, not as an image-generation failure. The summary is read-only and does not retry, clear, or change job checkpoints. `STORY-20261002-CB0614` has analysis ready and local images 1–2; its scene-3 pre-send check failed after an earlier prompt-ready observation. Three older Story jobs have no saved image checkpoints. A separate read-only WebView2 provider lab is tracked in `docs/plans/webview2-provider-prototype.md`.

The saved Story Shorts list now shows every pending ordinary Story in a bounded scroll area, retaining its scroll position across state refreshes. Each inactive card shows image progress from confirmed local scene files only, labeled as saved image scenes rather than overall job completion. The currently active card shows its matching live desktop job percent. Analysis, voice, video, and failed-scene status remain separate in the existing timeline. This display change does not modify the job or retry it.

The Story Shorts page presents “เริ่มเรื่องใหม่” and “งานเดิม” as adjacent tabs above the form. Selecting “งานเดิม” reveals the recovery cards directly, with no need to scroll past the long new-story form or expand another disclosure. The tab count follows the same pending-Story filter; an empty tab gives a clear message and leaves the finished-video Library separate.

For a later image-reference error without a scene number in `last_error`, the summary reads a bounded tail of the same job's trace and identifies the latest failed image attempt. It does not infer success from the trace and suppresses a failure once a later image checkpoint is saved. This source change needs an idle desktop restart before the already-open window displays it.

## Story resume tab and dispatch ownership (0.15.493, 2026-10-02)

When a Story resumes with a pending saved ChatGPT request, the desktop opens the exact saved conversation URL if Chrome is absent. The Extension reuses the one tab with that exact URL when the stored tab ID is stale, creates one only if absent, and stops before Start if multiple exact tabs make ownership ambiguous. The desktop records that a resume command was already queued across Extension heartbeat gaps; reconnect does not dispatch it a second time. An unconfirmed old image attachment stops automatic recovery before a fresh-tab retry. Saved analysis, images and uncertain provider Sends remain protected. Focused fixture evidence is distinct from live provider output.

## Automatic Story bootstrap draft recovery (0.15.492, 2026-10-02)

Fresh ChatGPT Story dispatch may encounter an older plain-text draft even in a newly created root tab. The Extension owns that new tab before any provider Start, clears only its text-only composer with document-ID fencing, verifies it stays empty after hydration, and then starts once. A changed adopted tab is left intact and replaced by one owned tab. Two bounded clear attempts cover a draft restored immediately after the first clear. No conversation, attachment, active response, prior send, or resume is cleared or replayed. On an unsafe or unready page, dispatch stops before Send with a logged reason. Installed pairing and live provider output are separate checks.

## Story bootstrap review (0.15.491 installed bridge verified, 2026-10-02)

The actual 490 run `STORY-20261002-895662` stopped before Send at 4% with `AI_WEB_WAIT_REVIEW`; the old cover draft remained visible in ChatGPT. The 490 trace did not distinguish a restored draft in a new tab from a draft hydrated late in an existing tab. For new Story analysis, Extension491 excludes previously owned automation tabs, confirms the same empty document twice after a bounded wait, and waits briefly for a late composer. A failed final check displays a review notice in Chrome and reports only a bounded cause and tab ID. It preserves drafts and sends nothing on failure. The DEV app and original Chrome Extension ID now report 0.15.491 and compatible; a real 491 Story has not yet been run.

## Story bootstrap tab guard (0.15.490 source candidate, 2026-10-02)

New ChatGPT Story analysis checks the exact root document before adopting an existing tab. It leaves tabs with drafts, attachments, conversations, or active responses untouched and opens one new tab if no clean root exists. A second check before content-script injection stops with `AI_WEB_WAIT_REVIEW` if that document changed. This prevents the old cover draft from being mistaken for a new Story composer. Saved Story/cover tabs, resumes, and provider Send receipts are not cleared or replayed. Desktop and Extension are now connected at `0.15.490`; a real new Story has not yet been run to verify provider behavior.

## Future idea: startup Chrome tab (2026-10-02)

Owner preference to revisit: the tab opened when SmartFlow starts could show SmartFlow's local web page (`/desktop/`) instead of `about:blank`. This is a note for future design, not an approved runtime change. Today `_start_browser_connection()` opens `about:blank` when Chrome is closed so the installed Extension can connect. Any later implementation should preserve the active browser profile, Extension connection, user tabs and drafts, and avoid adding a second SmartFlow tab when one is already open.

## Story Chrome tab and draft guard (2026-10-02)

When Chrome is already open, desktop focuses its window even if the Extension heartbeat has not connected yet; launching the provider URL in that gap created another tab. A new Story analysis preflight with `draft_changed` and no dispatched Send stops for review of the existing ChatGPT draft instead of automatically recovering into another tab. The original tab and draft remain untouched. Source activation requires restarting the SmartFlow developer app after active work ends.

## AI cover send diagnostics (2026-10-02)

Extension/Desktop 0.15.513 persists `native_retry_claim` for the exact ChatGPT stream-error panel. The claim binds request ID, conversation URL and user message ID, requires the accepted Send and verified source upload, and consumes the single Retry budget before any click. This also applies to a paused accepted cover resumed in collect-only mode. After a worker restart, the same claim permits result collection in the original one-user exchange without another Retry, Send or upload. Changed ownership, duplicate controls, quota text or missing proof remain review cases. Candidate 512 lacked the Desktop collector-stage acceptance and was superseded before activation.

Extension 0.15.512 recognizes ChatGPT's native message-stream error outside the assistant answer. For a fresh cover, it first durably records accepted ownership and a single Retry budget, then clicks the exact native Retry only while the latest prompt, conversation, control, empty draft and idle response still match. It waits for the same request's result without another Send or upload. A repeated error enters review with `AI_COVER_STREAM_ERROR`; collect-only recovery reports the error immediately and never uses provider Retry. The queue remains paused until a verified cover is saved.

Extension 0.15.511 cover recovery: after an accepted Send, ChatGPT may hide the reference attachment filenames in the user turn. A collect-only read of the original Extension-owned tab may use the desktop's request-bound verified upload proof when that conversation has exactly one user turn with the exact latest cover prompt, a stable conversation URL and a user message ID. Visible conflicting SmartFlow reference names, a retry turn, changed prompt, unconfirmed Send or incomplete proof still stop for review. Recheck the same ownership before downloading the assistant's image. No Send, upload, Retry or new tab occurs during this recovery; the queue still requires a validated saved image and ready ACK.

The cover collector reports a bounded heartbeat with the exact request's ownership, loaded image count, Stop visibility and time since answer content changed. A physically dispatched Send with an unknown acceptance remains on that same conversation and passively reads its result. The collector never uses that uncertainty to send another prompt. A loaded, exact owned cover may be saved after 60 seconds of stable display even if ChatGPT leaves Stop mounted; a changed image restarts the stability window. A genuinely active request without a usable cover reaches review after nine minutes, while an idle response still reaches review after six minutes. Existing video and cover receipts remain intact.

For Story checkpoint resume, a previous image request that was proven never sent may leave its named reference thumbnail in the composer after a failed Send preflight. The Extension removes that thumbnail only when the prepared receipt belongs to the exact job/scene, the same conversation has no sent request, the draft is empty or matches the exact wrapped prompt, no response is active, and a single named attachment has one Remove control. It then reattaches the saved checkpoint image. Unknown ownership, multiple attachments or a changed draft remain review cases.

The desktop cover ledger retains bounded Send target movement and trusted event evidence already reported by the Extension, without prompt text, coordinates, URLs or attachment bytes. A cover timeout with an unconfirmed Send and no image collector evidence identifies the Send acceptance stage; an accepted Send with no collector evidence identifies the result reader stage. Neither state proves a saved cover or grants an automatic retry. The existing request, completed video and generated scene images remain intact for explicit review or cover editing. This is a desktop-only diagnostic change; it does not change the Chrome click strategy or activate new Extension code.

## Story Shorts failure diagnostics (2026-10-01)

The existing Chrome Extension flow remains in use. New Story analysis prompts preserve user-specified ages but do not invent a child's numeric age or replace a requested fruit, animal, or object character with a human child. A confirmed ChatGPT image `policy_refusal` followed by a failed helper step is surfaced as `STORY_IMAGE_REFUSED`; the provider's generic refusal is not treated as proof of a specific forbidden subject. The desktop error report includes the job-owned Extension event timeline, the exact image prompt stored before Send with its acceptance status, and the saved analysis request marked as source material. It never automatically resends an accepted image request after a refusal. The durable source timeline remains at `workspace/stories/<job_id>/logs/extension_trace.jsonl`.

Story dispatch ownership: `LocalBridge.queue_extension_command` reuses an existing `open_story_chatgpt` command for the same Story Job and AI run while that command is pending, delivered, or ACKed as completed. ACK means the Extension accepted the command, not that ChatGPT analysis is finished. A late heartbeat or browser-launch recovery therefore cannot enqueue another master analysis in a second tab for the same run. After an engine restart, if the durable trace shows a ChatGPT request accepted but no analysis checkpoint, a fresh master request is blocked with `AI_WEB_WAIT_REVIEW`; the desktop keeps the AI tab open for review rather than closing it on this terminal. A new explicit run ID is distinct only when no accepted unsaved request exists. These guards do not repair jobs that already received duplicate requests before activation; preserve their tabs and receipts for review.

`RUN_DEV.bat` sets `SMARTFLOW_DEV_BYPASS_MEMBERSHIP=1` only for its child process. `production_membership` uses an in-memory development member only when the release is `development`, Python is not frozen, and the source checkout contains `.git` plus `.venv/pyvenv.cfg`. The UI labels this state `DEV MODE`; no membership token, vault credential, or membership server request is used. Normal `RUN.bat`, the customer release, and frozen builds keep the desktop membership check. This mode does not change Extension version matching, browser capability checks, job ownership, Send receipts, or provider login requirements.

### Local clip workflow history (schema 1)

`core/clip_run_history.py` observes successful Product/Story manifest writes plus Meta/scene receipt commits. It does not drive, retry, approve, migrate or resume work. `history/workflow.json` inside each job contains a deduplicated checkpoint timeline, selected providers/options fingerprints, saved scene/receipt states, known error codes plus opaque error keys, correction/recovery counts, source version/fingerprints and final-file size/mtime identity. Installed Extension version is explicitly `not_observed`, never inferred from the source requirement. No prompt, answer, free-form error, product title, URL, voice ID, token or account data is copied.

Story requires ready + video-ready + complete; Product requires automation-completed + video-ready; both require a confined nonempty final file and no cancellation/error. The resulting label is `completed_checkpoint`, NOT independent codec/visual/audio QA. Existing recorded video-QA status stays separate. Each distinct completion observation is snapshotted to immutable `history/success-<key>.json`; duplicates do not create another file. The live timeline is bounded to 512 events / approximately 4 MiB (retains at least its newest event); dropped count is explicit. Success snapshots keep their then-current bounded evidence; no claim of an exhaustive click trace. These diagnostics are best effort: I/O failure or a contended deletion/start guard never fails the provider job; errors are class-only logs. Deletion tombstones and current deletion claims are respected, and a history read never reconstructs a deleted/old job. History belongs to the job and is removed with it.

Library detail → file details → ประวัติการทำงานของคลิป shows the summary and opens only that job's history folder. Old jobs without a collector record show unavailable; no fabricated backfill. `tools/compare_clip_history.py <before.json> <after.json>` compares sanitized observations read-only; differences are not proof of causation. Before later fixes, inspect the selected failing run and a relevant preserved success, add the failing case as a regression, and preserve proven neighboring paths. Never restore a whole old source file based solely on a successful old version. Tests: `test_clip_run_history`, `clip_history_ui.cjs`, existing manager/receipt/library/deletion tests.

Versions/fingerprints identify code present at each observation, not the code that originally generated pre-existing media. A first observation of an already-complete job is only a completion snapshot, not a reconstructed run. Keep first-observed timestamp, event coverage and retained/dropped counts in every comparison. Unknown stage labels, unobserved browser actions and skipped/busy or failed writes must not be inferred as successes. Model/voice/options fingerprints let analysts detect selection changes without copying private values; inspect the original selected job only when more detail is necessary.

New Product Story captures opt into `analysis_recovery_version:1`; old jobs and queue snapshots are not migrated on reads/retry. The observed exact `{term,reading}` alias normalizes losslessly through the shared pronunciation contract, also for legacy editorial jobs. Raw owned completed text is stored before validation, separately from approved media checkpoints. An approval receipt commits in the same manifest write as approval; duplicate callbacks never allocate a new round. Shared required-field/count checks run before approval. Invalid format creates a revision-bound, field-allowlisted repair envelope with durable intent, 5/15/30/60-second backoff and repeated-issue strategy changes; no legacy two-round cutoff in this opt-in protocol. Unknown Send, changed ownership, cancellation, storage and account/policy blockers do not authorize provider resends. Storage retries only deliver the exact completed answer.

Existing serial v1/Flow speech/segments and per-scene provider selection remain unchanged. The proposed Product Meta serial v2 was tested in isolation but NOT included: explicit unfinished-scene selection created an enabled-yet-ineligible barrier. Preserve the working Meta↔Flow selection route until a unified scene adapter passes its full compatibility matrix. No wholesale existing-job adoption, fresh-context escalation or queue-wide account circuit is claimed by this first testable slice. Those broader plan items require separate owned lifecycle tests before enabling. Final/media validators are preserved, not bypassed. Source/fixture tests do not prove installed provider completion. See docs/reports/analysis-recovery-470.md.

## Runtime Extension 0.15.442 — completed Meta technical apology → new image and video prompt

For only the observed complete Thai Meta apology, the exact scene reader excludes visible toolbar-button text before strict full-reply classification. The desktop binds the saved source image, full original scene prompt and original ChatGPT/Gemini image provider to a one-owner redesign receipt. The helper requests a genuinely new feasible image prompt and matching video motion prompt without falsely calling this technical error a content-policy rejection. It creates one actual new image; desktop verifies bytes/aspect/distinctness, archives the old Meta attempt and starts only that scene in a fresh Meta context. Long Video's helper preserves landscape 16:9; Product/Shorts remain 9:16. Repeated confirmed failures can re-enter this saved transaction without resetting successful scenes. Generic server errors keep their prior same-input cooldown; policy/quota, unknown Send, active generation, late video, changed owner, draft and incomplete replies never authorize redesign. ## Runtime Extension 0.15.441 — Long Video recovery / Meta route evidence

Long Video's `#long-recovery-panel` renders unfinished `long_video` rows separately from Shorts, including image and provider clip counts. `MetaVideoManager.event` validates owner/context/cancellation before route actions. `route_recheck` persists a check ID, old Chrome document ID, expected conversation and cooldown (15–300s). `route_retry` requires a new document, ready empty Home, no draft/messages/media/activity/dialog/login, two stable samples over five seconds and elapsed cooldown. It archives only the failed scene receipt and allocates one successor with unchanged context; replayed ACK returns that same successor. `route_restored` removes a stale check when the owned conversation returns so its late video/activity is handled normally. Unknown Send/download and different conversation are not replay authority.

Meta errors read a uniquely matching failed scene receipt without restoring files, report expected vs observed URL distinctly and never substitute ChatGPT image completion. ## Passive automation liveness / 0.15.406

Runtime Extension 0.15.406 — paired desktop source, no installer. Source fault injection reproduced four failure groups: a transient monitor storage rejection permanently disarmed polling; stopped callbacks could rearm/stop a successor; an unacknowledged status/owner lookup blocked other outbox rows; completed download status was discarded on bridge failure; passive ChatGPT receipt-read failure consumed the only refresh opportunity.

Flow monitor epochs invalidate old callbacks before and after every awaited read/inspection. Its finally schedules only the same current watcher; ownership loss, Stop and legacy/Presenter limits still stop it. Transport failure alone is not native Flow failure and cannot authorize Generate or creative replacement.

The status-only outbox processes four fair concurrent rows with a durable cursor and exact per-key lock. Ownership checks/removal share that lock so a delayed old result cannot erase a newer report. Unacknowledged reports are kept, not converted into success or dropped by HTTP code. Download completion has a separate key; passive page status cannot overwrite its verified path. Old-version keys replay under their original keys. A download command ACK requires a delivered or durably queued completion; retries use the existing exact download receipt, not another provider action.

ChatGPT refresh preflight retries only passive storage reads, at most once per five seconds. Unsent user drafts/attachments veto reload without burning the future opportunity. Invalid receipts, actual dispatch, unknown reply and explicit denial retain the original one-shot rules and background durable reload budget. No changes to Send, image selection, prompts, settings, content-policy handling or successful media. See docs/reports/automation-liveness-406.md for verification and untested installed scope.

## Checkpoint resume / 0.15.259 — current contract

Runtime Extension 0.15.259 paired desktop; candidate pending installed workflow.

It does not click the provider Stop or navigate away; already accepted remote work may finish/use credits. Legacy reset behavior for other modes is unchanged. `pending_stop_commands` plus one durable re-pause budget reconcile missing memory-only ACKs after an EXE restart.
- `PresenterPipeline.wait` requires matching-version/run/shot, command ACK, then exact `flow_inspection_command_id` for an inspection. Background rejects previous inspection IDs. Only current `checkpoint_preparing` backed by same-run opening/preparing checkpoint and no submit/monitor/result/failure/confirmation can enter `resume_flow_workspace` once. Recheck same-project live evidence and wait workspace readiness read-only up to10s before arming. Unknown state stops after90s for review, not retry. Never reset intent merely because Chrome state is missing.
- `flow.js` preserves opening checkpoint before waiting for workspace, never downgrades active/complete evidence, reports actual composer readiness, and serializes generation inspections. Presenter inspections do not restore Generate tokens. Paused tab rejects mutating helper commands; downloads of the already generated result remain possible on explicit inspect/resume.
- `StoryManager.load_analysis_checkpoint` is the shared non-mutating save/package/resume validator. Optional description/bible/durations may default in memory; corrupt/wrong-job explicit files stop `STORY_ANALYSIS_CHECKPOINT_REVIEW`. Valid complete legacy manifest fallback is allowed only when the explicit checkpoint is absent. Existing voice reuse precedes new-TTS-only pronunciation validation, respecting needs_regeneration and actual nonempty in-job media.
- `chatgpt.js` restores indexed checkpoints with three read-only reads; failure stops `STORY_IMAGE_CHECKPOINT_UNREADABLE`. Durable Chrome `smartpostStoryGeneratedImage:{provider}:{job}:{scene}` records exact identity/claim before send and returned image URL before download. Same-image recovery is bounded3reads; no-response/stalled/unknown response does not resubmit. Receipt stays until exact scene save ACK. A confirmed no-image disposition can proceed only after an explicit saved scene edit with newer revision/new prompt and unchanged identity/style/sources; unknown/generated receipts cannot be bypassed. These receipts are Extension storage, not desktop disk backups, and can be lost on uninstall.
- Story analysis/image/final receiver checks current provider/tab/run and desktop run ownership before save; stale results cannot commit after a new run has been registered. AI resume keeps its registered provider tab without reload; a missing tab may open a fresh provider tab but never adopts an unrelated latest conversation. Existing Product prompts/physical send/Flow gestures and policy fallback remain unchanged.

## Previous contract delta — 0.15.256 Bounded Story name binding

- Installed255 job STORY-20260906-2A16D8 returned analysis but stopped before image1: third_signal was mapped to scene1 while the visible phrase described it without the canonical name. This was a lexical content guard failure, not a missing source or Flow failure.
- New, otherwise-valid Story/Drama plans may ask one text-only binding question for 1–12 missing non-user-anchored names. Reply must quote a unique existing phrase for every exact scene/entity pair. No new scenes, narration, registry, aliases or events are accepted. Local code inserts only the canonical name in parentheses and re-runs all validators. Invalid, uncertain, refused or ambiguous binding stops without image submission/retry.
- Saved/reviewed/paid plans are excluded. The desktop verifies exact before/after insertion proof and persists story_content_name_repair with the analysis checkpoint before image generation. Explicit later scene edits retain that proof in the original checkpoint and manifest history; effective reviewed prompts use their own existing revision/old-new audit, not stale automatic proof.
- Analysis instructions request canonical names beside descriptive phrases up-front. Progress records repairing_story_names, story_name_binding_response and story_names_repaired. Compact255 image briefs, source attachments, trusted Send, Flow physical actions and queue scheduling are unchanged. Source/desktop contract256; restart the EXE only when idle after installing256. Offline ten-scene execution is mocked, not installed provider E2E. Report: docs/reports/story-name-binding-20260906/VALIDATION.md.

## Previous contract delta — 0.15.250 Story content / visual medium

- Story/Drama style changes rendering only: linework, shading, light, color and materials. It never replaces named characters, costumes, setting, universe, relationships or scene events. `core/story_styles.py` is shared by Story, Batch and Drama. Original fiction stays original; named fictional characters are not automatically converted to unrelated substitutes.
- New jobs carry `story_content_contract.version = 1`, an explicit `story_entities` registry (id/name/aliases/visual_identity) and `scene_entities` per scene. Core and Extension check structure, entity references and names before persisting analysis or creating images. This is text/identity validation, NOT proof of visual fidelity or factual accuracy. Keep alleged/leaked-story uncertainty from the source.

Duplicate-image recovery changes framing/composition only, not the event/cast/location. A genuine Story image refusal stops with `STORY_IMAGE_REFUSED`; content mismatch stops with `STORY_CONTENT_MISMATCH`. Neither terminal silently substitutes characters or starts automatic desktop recovery.
- Existing manifests/checkpoints without the new contract remain legacy. Do not silently rewrite their plans, invalidate images/voice/Final, or charge for regeneration. Storyboard shows a read-only content review and legacy warnings alongside original files. A corrected visual plan for an existing paid/completed job requires a separate explicit revision workflow, not a schema migration that recreates media.
- Product scheduling, Google Flow physical actions, trusted AI Send lifecycle and 2,000-character sequential Voice remain unchanged. Program/Extension version contract is 0.15.250; installed visual smoke remains pending until the user loads the matching Extension and restarts the EXE when idle.

## Previous contract delta — 0.15.249 AI send lifecycle

- `CLICK_AI_SEND_BUTTON`: strict Job/Run/tab ownership and per-tab single-flight; settle focus before mouse-down; resolve/hit-test the exact live draft and Send node after hover and require stable geometry/node identity before the single press. One release at the same coordinates, including cleanup when the press response is lost. No retry click/Enter/retarget.
- Just before arming the gesture, `VERIFY_AI_SEND_READY` asks the active content helper to recheck its current Job/Run, cancellation flag and exact draft; cancellation received during preparation stops before press.
- Scoped capture records the full gesture even when release misses Send. Trusted click is proved only against the latched original node/event path. Optional bounded diagnostics: `gesture_phase`, `target_changed`, `release_on_send_target`, `target_stable_before_press`; Bridge and copied error log preserve these without arbitrary DOM content.
- `dispatched:true` now attests a started press, not necessarily completed release; `dispatchCompleted`/`gesture_phase` distinguish uncertainty. Content always checks existing passive acceptance and retains the draft after 60 seconds unconfirmed, never sends again.
- `INSTALL_EXTENSION.bat` opens canonical `browser_extension` only after manifest/desktop/release versions match; no hardcoded historical folder. Source/package/version are 0.15.249. Physical Google Flow behavior and sequential Voice chunking remain unchanged.
- Incident `STORY-20260905-16F867`: 10/15 source images saved; unconfirmed send for image 11. Down-only legacy capture cannot prove absent browser release. Focus/hover lifecycle is a reproduced risk, not proven as this live incident's sole cause. Installed Chrome smoke remains pending.
- `story_error` previously auto-closed the AI tab immediately after reporting that its draft was preserved (21:28:40 error / 21:28:41 close). Current same-Job compatible-Extension error with sanitized unconfirmed-send evidence now keeps that tab and bypasses auto-recovery. It still marks failure/updates queue and popup normally. Explicit cancel/success and unrelated-error cleanup remain unchanged. Guard: `tests/test_story_ai_send_draft.py`.

## 1. เป้าหมายและขอบเขต

SmartFlow AI เป็นโปรแกรม Windows สำหรับทำคอนเทนต์ Shopee Affiliate, Story Shorts และละครสั้นแบบหลาย EP โดยเชื่อมงาน 4 ฝั่ง:

```text
Google Chrome Extension
  ├─ Shopee Affiliate → นำข้อมูล/รูปสินค้าเข้า Product Job
  ├─ ChatGPT Web / Gemini Web → บท, แคปชั่น, Prompt และรูป (ไม่ใช้ API key)
  └─ Google Flow → ปกติสร้างคลิปจริงทีละ source; policy-terminal slot ใช้ local motion แบบมี provenance
             ↓ Local Bridge 127.0.0.1:8765
SmartPost Hybrid Desktop (HTML/CSS/JS บน Edge WebView2)
  ├─ Product Mode → Video AI clips → Voice → Subtitle → Logo → Audio → Android
  └─ Story Mode → ภาพต่อเนื่อง → Motion ในเครื่อง หรือ Google Flow ทีละฉาก → Voice/Subtitle/Logo/Audio → Shorts
  └─ Drama Series → Character Bible → EP Queue → Story engine เดิม → Shorts หลายตอน
             ↓
Android ผ่าน ADB/scrcpy (Dry Run และ Confirm เปิดเป็นค่าเริ่มต้น)
```

ข้อแยกสำคัญ:

- Product Mode ที่ใช้ `video_ai_provider=flow` มีหนึ่ง ordered segment ต่อรูปต้นทาง: ปกติเป็นคลิปจริงจาก Google Flow แต่ structured current-card `FLOW_POLICY_BLOCKED` ครั้งแรกใช้รูป canonical ของ slot เดิมสร้าง local motion ทันที แล้วไป source ถัดไป ห้าม safe-prompt retry, reupload, reassign หรือ Alternate Take Final ที่มี local segment ใช้ `google_flow_hybrid_composite` พร้อมจำนวน Flow/local/ทั้งหมดตามจริง ห้ามเรียก local segment ว่าคลิป Flow
- Story Mode มี 2 ทางเลือกที่ผู้ใช้ต้องเลือกชัดเจน: `image_motion` ใช้ภาพต่อเนื่องและ `video_source_type=story_image_sequence`; `google_flow` ส่งภาพทุกฉากเข้า Flow ทีละรูปและปกติจบเป็น `google_flow_story_composite` หาก Story/Drama ฉากหนึ่งได้ structured current-card `FLOW_POLICY_BLOCKED` ครั้งแรก ให้ทำ local motion จากรูป canonical เดิมทันที เดินฉากถัดไป และระบุ Final เป็น `google_flow_story_hybrid_fallback` พร้อม `flow_clip_count`, `flow_fallback_count` และรายการฉากสำรอง ห้าม safe-prompt retry/reupload/Alternate Take และห้ามรายงานฉากสำรองว่าเป็นคลิป Flow จริง
- การสร้างรูปเลือกใช้หน้า `chatgpt.com` หรือ `gemini.google.com` ใน Google Chrome ผ่าน Extension ไม่ใช้ ImageGen ของ Codex และไม่ใช้ OpenAI/Gemini API key
- หยุดเมื่อเจอ CAPTCHA, OTP, สิทธิ์รูป หรือหน้าความปลอดภัยที่บริการบังคับให้เจ้าของบัญชียืนยัน

## 2. จุดเริ่มโปรแกรม

### Main UI / creator-ux-1 — 2026-09-09

Source-only UX change; no installer or patch built. `web_ui/creator_ux.js/css` adds six pre-create/queue summaries, complete five-kind new-job menu, clear settings scope, responsive audio/form layout and reduced-motion support. `media_audio.js` preserves independent subtitle/music/SFX selections when switching main audio; invalid Flow source still rejected by existing gate. Legacy subtitle labels are explicitly hidden with a scoped data attribute (existing display CSS overrode HTML hidden). `usability.js` long-video queue shortcut navigates to the shared queue rather than triggering another page's hidden toggle; Flow help reflects bounded recovery without promising success. No backend job mutation or Extension change. Existing popup minimize ownership retained.

Validation: 25 focused Python tests, 1030 full tests; `tests/creator_ux_smoke.js` against isolated EXE checks menu/summaries, audio preservation, queue inheritance, minimized popup heartbeat, 6 pages at 4 viewport sizes (including approximate scaled sizes), no automation POST. `tests/media_audio_ui_smoke.js` checks payloads for 5 creation routes and queue editing. Screenshots in `build/creator-ux-smoke`. Real Windows scaling and user-filled library/job workflows still need manual review; this is not a claim to have redesigned every page. Customer beta.8 installer does not contain this later UI change.

Remaining UX plan: complete saved-job status/step labels from persisted audio choices, all-page form grouping, detailed library/version distinction and actual Windows scaling review. Do not infer current job settings from a new-job form or reset generation state while doing presentation work.

### Customer installer / 0.3.0-beta.8 — 2026-09-09

Bundled audio: `tools/build_customer.py` now includes `assets/audio/background` (4 tracks) and `assets/audio/sfx` (13 effects). Frozen `prepare_customer_root` seeds missing supported audio files into the editable customer library, including upgrades from a package missing audio. Existing same-name and user-added files are never overwritten. Source-mode, mixing choices, queue snapshots and Extension 0.15.276 are unchanged. Regression: `test_bundled_audio_seeds_and_preserves_customer_files`. Online publishing remains pending; bundled track redistribution rights require review before a public commercial release.

See `docs/CUSTOMER_INSTALLER.md` for build, release key, installer, data separation and deployment status. Frozen customer startup uses `core/customer_runtime.py`; source checkout ROOT is unchanged. `desktop/hybrid.py` starts frozen children with `--engine`, isolated WebView profile and loaded-window receipt. Build packages the complete FFmpeg shared DLL set, never workstation config/jobs/credentials. Extension remains0.15.276 with no generation changes.

`core/app_updates.py`, `desktop/update_api.py`, `tools/customer_updater.py`, `web_ui/updates.js` implement signed release checks, native download controls, actual Extension version display and external patch application. `prepare_app_update` reuses creation idle/remote checks and pauses FIFO without resubmitting work; blocks new desktop actions except shutdown. Initial beta has no supported old install versions until installed upgrade/rollback tests pass. Host API/admin source is prepared but not published; original frontend build restored pending API availability. Full public readiness must not be inferred from packaging/unit tests.

| ส่วน | ไฟล์หลัก | หน้าที่ |
|---|---|---|
| Launcher | `RUN.bat`, `app.py`, `launcher/resolve_python.ps1` | เปิด Hybrid Desktop เท่านั้น; RUN และ EXE ใช้ Python resolver แหล่งเดียว ไม่มีทางเข้า UI รุ่นเก่า |
| Desktop host | `desktop/hybrid.py` | เปิด pywebview/Edge WebView2, ดูแล hidden engine และปิด child process ตามหน้าต่าง |
| Web UI | `web_ui/index.html`, `styles.css`, `app.js` | หน้าหลัก responsive, one-click, คลังวิดีโอ, คู่มือ และ Log |
| Hidden Engine | `ui/main_window.py` | pipeline, event queue และ workers ที่ทำงานเบื้องหลังโดยไม่แสดงหน้าต่าง Tk |
| Atomic state | `core/atomic_json.py` | lock ข้าม thread/process, unique temp, flush/fsync, replace retry และ last-known-good backup นอกโฟลเดอร์งาน |
| Config | `config.json`, `core/config.py`, `core/customer_runtime.py` | Source checkout ที่ไม่มี config ใช้ safe defaults ในหน่วยความจำ; การบันทึกค่าครั้งแรกสร้างไฟล์ผ่าน shared Atomic JSON transaction. ไฟล์เดิมและไฟล์เสียหายไม่ถูกแทนที่ด้วย defaults |
| Local Bridge | `core/local_bridge.py` | HTTP ระหว่างโปรแกรมกับ Chrome Extension |
| Chrome | `browser_extension/` | Shopee, ChatGPT/Gemini Web และ Google Flow automation |

## 3. หน้า GUI

### Shopee unfinished-job sidebar / desktop product-jobs-2

`web_ui/product_continue.js/css` keeps the create form first and moves unfinished Shopee jobs to a compact right sidebar; <=1100px becomes a collapsed section below the form. Pending/hidden/trash tabs, individual or selected-item trash, explicit confirmation, restore, and broken-thumbnail placeholder. Resume uses the exact saved JOB/STORY identity/provider/settings; no new-form values or provider action changes.

`ui/product_jobs.py` validates all selected product jobs before mutation, blocks active local/cover/browser/queued Extension work (including disconnected nonterminal ownership), and rejects completed or unrelated Drama/Story jobs. `CreationQueue.manage_product_jobs` commits `product_job_controls` tombstones and cancelled queue rows atomically in the existing queue file. No media/checkpoint/manifest deletion. Hide affects this sidebar only; restore does not automatically enqueue. Queue retry/import/claim/startup and manual or timed recovery cannot revive a trashed job. Late result bytes remain preserved; independent tombstones keep the job excluded. Extension0.15.370 source unchanged; desktop bridge adds only read-only pending-command inspection. Tests `test_product_job_management`, `test_product_continue` and `product_continue_ui.js`; installed E2E status in `docs/reports/product-jobs-sidebar.md`.

### Drama queue resume — 2026-09-09 (desktop-only)

`web_ui/app.js:toggleDramaQueue` calls existing `story_queue_resume/pause` directly, never clicks the Shorts-only button (disabled when all Shorts are complete). Uses a pending-request latch and actual Drama queued/running rows for remaining EP count and enablement. This still controls the shared FIFO, not a new per-series scheduler. No automatic resume on startup; no new jobs or EP1 replay.

`core/drama_series.py:_recovered_series_status` derives stale needs_attention as queued/completed only when the complete episode inventory contains a completed EP and otherwise only queued/completed states. get/list are read-only, so legacy manifests and media are untouched; mark_episode_ready persists this correction for newly recovered EPs. Failed/running/cancelled/unknown states and cancelled series are preserved. Extension0.15.275 unchanged. Regression: tests/test_drama_queue_resume.py and tests/drama_queue_resume_harness.js. Installed EXE smoke remains pending.

### Studio review delta — 2026-09-05 (Desktop-only, Extension 0.15.243 unchanged)

- `web_ui/studio.js` / `studio.css` แยกการตรวจงานออกจาก UI หลัก: Dashboard แสดงงานปัจจุบันก่อนสถิติ, Story/Drama เปิด Storyboard รายฉากแบบ on-demand, Log มีตัวกรอง Job/บริการ/error และคัดลอกเฉพาะที่กรอง
- `core/studio_review.py` ทำ read model จากไฟล์ที่อยู่ใน Job จริงเท่านั้น: scene image/Flow/local/Final ต้องตรวจไฟล์และ path containment ก่อนแสดง ไม่สร้างภาพ/วิดีโอหรือคิดเวลาปลอม
- actions `story_review`, `story_preview_pronunciation`, `story_save_pronunciation` คืนเฉพาะงานที่เลือก ไม่ต้องโหลด Library/ทุก Job การตรวจคำอ่านไม่เรียก AI Voice; บันทึก `user_pronunciation_notes` แยกจากคำแนะนำ AI และใช้ร่วม Voice/Subtitle
- การเขียนคำอ่านตรวจ manifest revision ใต้ AtomicJsonFile lock; active/running/cancelled และงานที่มี paid Voice ID/เสียง/Dialogue checkpoint ห้ามแก้ทับ บันทึกคำอ่านไม่ clear paid checkpoint/ภาพ ไม่ส่งงานซ้ำและไม่ประกาศ Final
- `story-scene:JOB:INDEX:KIND` ผ่าน media adapter เท่านั้น; ห้ามรับ absolute path ภายนอก Job หรือเดาตำแหน่งจาก compact partial image array
- `mode=logs` รวม compact heartbeat กับ Log ล่าสุด ไม่สแกน Library; เปลี่ยนตัวกรองใช้ข้อมูลในหน้า ไม่เรียก backend ทุก keystroke
- `save_analysis_checkpoint` บันทึก `voice_review_issues` ก่อนสร้างภาพ แต่ไม่ทิ้ง Analysis เพราะมีคำรอตรวจ; ผู้ใช้ตรวจ/แก้ได้จาก Storyboard เมื่อหยุดงาน
- Animation ใหม่หยุดเมื่อหน้าต่างซ่อน และตาม reduced-motion; Dialog คืน focus ให้ปุ่มต้นทาง ผลตรวจที่ `docs/reports/studio-20260905-phase2/`
- รอบนี้ไม่แก้หรือแพ็ก Extension ใหม่: ยังใช้ 0.15.243 ตาม CURRENT_RELEASE

หน้าหลักใช้ Hybrid Desktop: HTML/CSS/JavaScript ใน `web_ui/` แสดงผ่าน `pywebview` และ Microsoft Edge WebView2 ส่วน Python/Tk เดิมทำหน้าที่เป็น engine ที่ซ่อนอยู่ เพื่อรักษา pipeline และ event queue ที่ผ่านการทดสอบแล้ว ผู้ใช้จึงได้ UI แบบเว็บที่ลื่นและ responsive โดยไม่ต้องย้าย automation ทั้งระบบในครั้งเดียว

`LocalBridge` พอร์ตเดิม `127.0.0.1:8765` เป็นเจ้าของทั้ง Extension API และ Desktop UI เพื่อหลีกเลี่ยงพอร์ตที่สองและไม่สร้าง FastAPI process ซ้ำ: `/desktop/` ให้ไฟล์ UI, `/api/desktop/state` อ่าน snapshot, `/api/desktop/action` ส่งคำสั่งเข้า Tk thread ผ่าน queue และ `/api/desktop/media` ส่งเฉพาะไฟล์ preview ที่ตรวจ path แล้ว การเปิดจาก origin ภายนอกห้ามส่ง desktop action ข้ามเว็บ

ตัว host ใน `desktop/hybrid.py` ต้องเปิด hidden engine แบบ `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, ผูก child process กับ Windows Job Object แบบ `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE` และสั่ง shutdown เมื่อปิดหน้าต่าง เพื่อไม่ให้ CMD, Python หรือพอร์ต 8765 ค้างอยู่ Hidden Engine ต้องใช้ชื่อหน้าต่าง `SmartFlow AI — Hidden Engine` และตัวตรวจ single-instance ต้อง focus เฉพาะหน้าต่าง Hybrid ที่มองเห็นจริง ห้ามดึง Tk Engine ที่ถอนหน้าต่างไว้กลับมาแสดงเป็น UI รุ่นเก่า หาก WebView2/pywebview ใช้ไม่ได้ `app.py` ต้องแจ้งข้อผิดพลาดให้ผู้ใช้แก้ไข ห้าม fallback ไปเปิด UI รุ่นเก่า

ระบบภาพใช้ Professional Creator Studio design system: พื้นหลัง dark navy, surface หลายระดับ, สี cyan/blue/purple, card/modal/toast/progress และ sidebar responsive หน้าจอแคบใช้เมนู slide-over โดยต้องไม่มี horizontal overflow

เครดิตบริการต้องเห็นง่ายโดยไม่ต้องเปิดหน้าตั้งค่า: แถบบนทุกหน้ามีตัวเลขย่อของ AI Voice/AI Subtitle, หน้า `dashboard` มีการ์ดเครดิตขนาดใหญ่ และหน้า `voice`/`subtitle` มีแถบยอดคงเหลือของบริการนั้น หากไม่มี credential ให้แสดง `ยังไม่เชื่อม` พร้อมทางลัดไปหน้าตั้งค่า; หากเชื่อมแล้วแต่ตรวจไม่ได้ให้แสดง `ตรวจเครดิตไม่ได้` ห้ามแสดงยอดเดาหรือค้างยอดเก่าเป็นสถานะพร้อม

หน้า Story เป็นหน้าต้นแบบของโครงใหม่: แถบสรุป 4 ขั้น, ฟอร์มหัวข้อและรายละเอียด, ตัวปรับจำนวนฉากพร้อมค่าปัจจุบัน, รูปหลักแบบไม่บังคับ, ปุ่มอัตโนมัติหลักเพียงปุ่มเดียว, การ์ดงานล่าสุด และผลจาก ChatGPT Web โดยไม่เปลี่ยนลอจิก pipeline เดิม

หน้า Hybrid sidebar เรียงดังนี้:

1. `dashboard` ภาพรวม Bridge, Extension, Voice, Subtitle และผลงานล่าสุด
2. `products` โหมด Affiliate/คลิปปักตะกร้า: วางลิงก์ เลือก ChatGPT/Gemini, one-click, บันทึกลิงก์ไว้ก่อน, ตารางเฉพาะ Product Job ที่ต้องทำต่อ และแผงเครื่องมือซ่อมเฉพาะขั้นตอนแบบยุบได้; ทางลัด Voice, Subtitle และ Logo ใช้ Sidebar/หน้าหลักของบริการนั้นเพียงจุดเดียว
3. `story` หัวข้อ/แนวทาง/รูปหลัก/จำนวนฉาก และเริ่ม Story one-click
4. `drama` ละครสั้นแบบซีรีส์: ชื่อเรื่อง/โครงเรื่อง, ตัวละคร 1–2 คนพร้อมรูปอ้างอิง, provider, จำนวน EP/ฉาก และ one-click ทั้งซีรีส์
5. `library` คลังเฉพาะวิดีโอผลงานหลัก พร้อม popup ชื่อ แคปชั่น แฮชแท็ก ลิงก์ ดูวิดีโอ เปิดโฟลเดอร์, ลบรายวิดีโอ/รายโปรเจกต์, ลบวิดีโอทั้งหมด และลบโปรเจกต์ที่เสร็จแล้วทั้งหมด
6. `video` AI provider, ความละเอียด, FPS, คุณภาพ, motion และ transition
7. `voice` Product Job, API Key, voice catalog/reference, ภาษา/อารมณ์/ความเร็ว, บทพูด และสร้างเสียง
8. `subtitle` Token, Product Job, 1–5 พยาง, ธีม/animation/font, ระยะวรรณยุกต์ไทย, ตำแหน่ง สี พรีวิวจริง และ render
9. `audio` เพลงพื้นหลัง/SFX แบบอัตโนมัติ สุ่ม เลือกเอง เพิ่มไฟล์ และ Short Mix V2 พร้อมกำหนดความยาวท่อน/ระดับ Voice Ducking
10. `logo` คลังโลโก้ถาวรใน `assets/logos/user`, อัปโหลด PNG/JPG/WEBP, คลิกเลือก, สถานะตัวที่เลือก, บันทึกค่าเริ่มต้น, ปรับความโปร่งใส/ขนาด/ระยะขอบ/ตำแหน่ง, พรีวิวจริง และ render
11. `queue` คิววิดีโอมือถือ Android, Dry Run, ยืนยันก่อนโพสต์, ADB/scrcpy/Shopee/UI hierarchy
12. `guide` คู่มือเริ่มต้น, ตรวจความพร้อม และวิธีติดตั้ง Chrome Extension แบบ Load unpacked
13. `logs` กิจกรรมล่าสุดและ Log สด

Hybrid UI ต้องมีเครื่องมือประจำวันครบทั้ง 13 หน้า สำหรับ Affiliate/คลิปปักตะกร้า, Story, Drama, Voice, Subtitle, Audio, Logo และคิวมือถือ ส่วน Tk เดิมคงไว้เฉพาะ Hidden Engine/worker/checkpoint เบื้องหลังเท่านั้น ไม่มีปุ่ม ไฟล์ หรือพารามิเตอร์สำหรับเปิดเป็นหน้าจอผู้ใช้ ห้ามสร้าง pipeline ชุดใหม่แยกกัน เพราะจะทำให้ checkpoint และ worker แข่งกัน

การลบใน Hybrid ต้องครอบคลุมลบวิดีโอรายงาน/ทั้งคลัง, ลบโปรเจกต์ที่เลือก, ลบ Product Job ทั้งหมด และลบโปรเจกต์ที่เสร็จแล้วทั้งหมด ทุกคำสั่งต้องยืนยันใน UI ใหม่ก่อนและย้ายไฟล์ลงถังขยะ Windows; ห้ามลบถาวรหรือเปิดกล่องยืนยันของ Legacy ซ้อนอีกชั้น

หน้า `guide` เป็น Setup Center สำหรับผู้ใช้ทั่วไป: แสดงสถานะ Local Bridge, Extension รุ่นที่รองรับ, AI Voice และ AI Subtitle จากค่าจริง พร้อมปุ่มเปิด `chrome://extensions`, เปิด/คัดลอกโฟลเดอร์ `browser_extension`, เปิดเว็บที่ต้องล็อกอิน และลิงก์ไปหน้าตั้งค่าที่เกี่ยวข้อง การติดตั้ง Extension ยังต้องให้ผู้ใช้กด `Developer mode` และ `Load unpacked` เองตามข้อกำหนดความปลอดภัยของ Chrome

ปุ่ม `ตรวจระบบอีกครั้ง` ใน Hybrid ต้องเรียก `_refresh_guide_status()` ผ่าน action `check_system_readiness` และคืนค่า `ready_count` จริง ห้ามใช้เพียง action `refresh` เพราะ Footer/Guide จะค้างค่า 3/4 แม้ Extension กลับมาออนไลน์แล้ว

หน้า `logs` ต้องมีแถบ `LIVE ACTIVITY` และ Console ที่สะท้อนงาน Product/Story/Voice/Subtitle/Flow/Render จริง พร้อมเวลา, Job, เปอร์เซ็นต์, ขั้นตอน และสิ่งที่กำลังรอ ข้อความเดิมที่ซ้ำภายในช่วงสั้นต้องกรองออก และจำกัดจำนวนบรรทัดในหน่วยความจำเพื่อไม่ให้โปรแกรมช้าหลังเปิดใช้งานนาน

Hybrid Log ต้องรวมทั้งไฟล์ log ล่าสุดและข้อความสดใน `self.console`; ห้ามแสดงเพียงไฟล์เก่าจนกิจกรรมล่าสุด เช่น ADB/Voice/Subtitle/Render ปรากฏเฉพาะแถบสถานะ แต่ไม่ปรากฏใน Console

## 4. Product Mode

### 4.1 ลำดับงาน

```text
ลิงก์ Shopee Affiliate
→ workspace/products/JOB-*/job.json
→ รูป/ข้อมูลสินค้า
→ ChatGPT Web สร้าง 3 รูป + 3 Flow prompts + caption + spoken_script
→ One-click ตรวจโครงสร้างและอนุมัติสำหรับงานตัดต่ออัตโนมัติ
→ Google Flow สร้างและดาวน์โหลด flow_shot_NN.mp4 ตามปกติ; structured policy-terminal slot ใช้ product_local_motion_shot_NN.mp4 จากรูป canonical เดิม
→ MultiFlowComposer รวม ordered source ครบ target และบันทึก all-Flow/hybrid provenance ตามจริง
→ Voice / Subtitle / Effects / Logo / Background / SFX
→ readiness ผ่าน
→ ส่งมือถือ/โพสต์ตาม Safe Mode
```

- หน้า Product มีเส้นทางหลักปุ่มเดียว `สร้างคลิปขายอัตโนมัติจนเสร็จ`: ผู้ใช้วางลิงก์ เลือก ChatGPT/Gemini และตัวเลือก Subtitle แล้วกดครั้งเดียว
- ตาราง Product เปิด `Product Video Detail` เมื่อคลิกแถวหนึ่งครั้ง โดยแสดงรูปปก ชื่อวิดีโอ ชื่อไฟล์ Final แคปชั่นพร้อมแฮชแท็ก และ `posting_product_url` พร้อมปุ่มดูวิดีโอ เปิดโฟลเดอร์ เปิดหน้าสินค้า และคัดลอกข้อมูล; ถ้าไฟล์ Final ยังไม่พร้อมต้องปิดใช้งานปุ่มดูวิดีโอและแสดงสถานะให้ชัดเจน
- ปุ่มทำทีละขั้นยังอยู่ใต้หัวข้อ `เครื่องมือทำต่อ / แก้เฉพาะขั้นตอน` เพื่อกู้หรือแก้บางช่วง ไม่ใช่เส้นทางหลักของผู้ใช้ทั่วไป
- One-click ต้องตั้ง `automation_status=running` ก่อนส่ง AI Web; เมื่อ `/api/ai/result` กลับมา Local Bridge ห้าม queue `open_flow` ซ้ำ เพราะ pipeline เป็นเจ้าของลำดับ Flow ทั้ง 3 ช็อต
- การอ่าน/แก้/บันทึก JSON หลักต้องผ่าน `core/atomic_json.py`: ครอบ transaction ด้วย re-entrant lock ใน process และ file lock ข้าม process, ใช้ unique temp ในโฟลเดอร์เดียวกัน, flush/fsync, `os.replace` แบบ retry และสำรอง last-known-good ไว้ที่ `backups/runtime-state`; ห้ามอ่านไฟล์เสียแล้วคืน Job/Queue ว่าง เพราะ UI, Extension callback และ worker อาจทำพร้อมกันจนข้อมูล Checkpoint หาย
- ก่อนอนุมัติอัตโนมัติต้องมีภาพจริง 3 รูป, Flow prompt 3 ชุด, caption, spoken script และไฟล์ภาพทุกไฟล์อยู่จริง; ถ้าไม่ครบให้ Resume AI Web Job เดิม
- Checkpoint ตรวจจากไฟล์จริง: ข้ามภาพ เสียง Subtitle และ Flow shot ที่เสร็จแล้ว โดยเฉพาะ Flow 1–2 ต้องเริ่มต่อที่ช็อต 3 ห้ามย้อนสร้างใหม่ทั้งหมด
- ปุ่ม `ทำต่อ` ของ Product Job ต้องใช้ `image_ai_provider` ที่บันทึกใน Job เดิม ไม่ใช้ dropdown ล่าสุดไปทับ; ถ้า AI+เสียงพร้อมแต่วิดีโอยังไม่ครบ ต้องเปิด Chrome ไปยัง Google Flow ของช็อตแรกที่ขาดทันที ห้ามสลับ ChatGPT/Gemini อัตโนมัติทุกกรณี การเปลี่ยน Provider ต้องมาจากคำสั่งผู้ใช้หรือการสร้าง Job ใหม่เท่านั้น
- Product AI บันทึก `generated/selling_image_XX.png` ทันทีหลังได้แต่ละภาพผ่าน `/api/jobs/partial-image`; ถ้า ChatGPT/Gemini ไม่ส่งรูปใหม่ภายใน 6 นาที โปรแกรมใช้ Recovery สูงสุด 2 รอบ โดยใช้ Analysis JSON เดิมและส่งเฉพาะ Prompt ของรูปที่ยังขาด ห้ามลบหรือสร้าง Checkpoint เดิมซ้ำ
- Recovery ชั้นที่ 2 ของ AI Web/การสร้างภาพต้องหยุดและขอให้ผู้ใช้จัดการเองเมื่อเป็น Login, CAPTCHA, เครดิต, Permission, image-policy หรือการยืนยันสิทธิ์ และต้องแสดง `กู้คืนชั้นที่ 2 n/2` พร้อมจำนวนภาพจริงใน Popup/Log; ข้อนี้ไม่ใช่กฎ Flow policy ซึ่งใช้ immediate local fallback ตาม `0.15.242`
- Recovery รอบแรกและรอบถัดไปใช้ provider ที่บันทึกใน Job เดิมเท่านั้น: `resume_chatgpt` หรือ `restart_chatgpt_images` เป็นชื่อคำสั่ง legacy แต่ต้องส่ง `provider_hint` เดิมเสมอ ห้ามเปิด provider สำรองเอง และต้องใช้ Analysis/ภาพ Checkpoint เดิมเพื่อสร้างเฉพาะรูปที่ขาด
- ถ้า error เกิดก่อน Analysis JSON หรือภาพใดถูกบันทึกจริง Recovery ต้องวิเคราะห์ใหม่ใน Job เดิมผ่าน provider เดิม ห้ามใช้ `resume_chatgpt` แล้วจบด้วย `ไม่พบผลวิเคราะห์เดิม`; เมื่อมี Analysis/partial checkpoint จริงจึงใช้ Resume/Restart แบบไม่สร้างซ้ำ
- คำสั่ง Flow ของช็อตใหม่เริ่มจาก `https://flow.google.com/` แล้วกด `โปรเจ็กต์ใหม่`; แต่ Resume/Retry ของ Job+ช็อตเดิมต้องใช้ `smartpostFlowActiveProject` และแท็บ `/project/` เดิม ห้ามเปิดแท็บหรือโปรเจกต์ใหม่เมื่อรูป/Prompt อยู่ใน Workspace แล้ว
- Golden Flow 2026: อัปโหลดรูปเข้าโปรเจกต์เพียงครั้งเดียว → รอจน media card พร้อม → รีเฟรชหนึ่งครั้ง → ตั้ง `ยืนยันก่อนสร้าง=ไม่เลย`, วิดีโอ `9:16`, จำนวน `1x` → เปิด `เพิ่มองค์ประกอบลงในช่องพรอมต์` → เลือกรูปที่อัปโหลด → ตรวจว่ารูปและ Prompt อยู่ใน Composer เดียวกัน → กด `เริ่มสร้าง` ครั้งเดียว ห้ามรีเฟรช/แนบรูป/ส่ง Prompt ซ้ำระหว่างเรนเดอร์
- การอัปโหลดรูปเข้า `สื่อทั้งหมด` ยังไม่ใช่หลักฐานว่าแนบรูปแล้ว: ต้องกดปุ่ม `aria-haspopup="dialog"` ข้าง Composer เลือก media card ที่ชื่อ `selling_image_XX`/`flow-reference` และตรวจ thumbnail/removal control ใน Composer ก่อนกด `สร้าง`; ถ้ารูปอยู่ในคลังแล้วห้ามอัปโหลดซ้ำ
